# C-STS training exclusions after full review

Every entry has a final semantic disposition. Split conflicts are distinct from label invalidity. These decisions are assistant annotations; explicitly user-confirmed policy boundaries are documented in the main report.

## 1. train_24: type of building

**Status:** invalid. **Original label:** 2.0. **Final rating:** None.

**Caption A:** A tub is sitting in the middle of the room while the curtains are drawn open.

**Caption B:** Big bed with a roof has a mirror on the center of bed roof

**Scope:** type or function of the building

**A value:** tub room; building function unspecified

**B value:** bedroom; building function unspecified

**Decision:** Room furnishings establish room use, not the type of the containing building; residential and commercial accommodation remain unestablished.
